Let's design smooth objective for softmax parameter y:
`def softmax_objective(y, flag)`:
- read global `WS`.
- compute x = S * exp(y - max)/sum.
- compute a_i = base_i * x_block_i / B_i. Note if base_i zero? base positive.
- Compute c = fft_convolve_self(a)
- If flag==0: value = LSE(c,beta) maybe normalized? For optimization, use raw LSE (units of p). Since p ~ O(1), LSE ~ logsum. We can include constant c_max + logsum/β. LSE smooth.
- Gradient wrt y:
   Compute grad_a = fft_grad_lse(a,c,beta) (for LSE wrt a).
   grad_x = bincount(block_id, weights=grad_a*base, minlength=K)/B.
   gy = x * (grad_x - np.dot(grad_x, x)/S).
- Return value, [gradient] if flag.

`fft_convolve_and_grad_lse(a,beta)`:
```
size=2n-1; m=1...; A=fft(a,m); c=real(ifft(A*A)); maxc=c.max(); shifted=c-maxc; e=exp(beta*shifted); total=e.sum(); if total==0: w=zeros else w=e/total; value=maxc+log(total)/beta

```

```
def run_softmax_pipeline(base, n, time_budget, rng, label):
    base = normalize(base, n)
    current_base=base
    Ks_candidates=[8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048]
    Ks=[k for k in Ks_candidates if k< n or (k==n and final direct maybe)]
    betas=[15,50,150]
    stages=[]
    for K in Ks: for beta in betas: stages.append((K,beta))
    if len(stages)>0:
       time_per = max(5, (budget*0.9)/len(stages))
         for K,beta in stages:
            if time > deadline - reserve or not enough: break
            base_for = current_base
            timeout = min(time_per, deadline - time - reserve)
            best_a, score = run_softmax_lbfgs(base_for, K, beta, timeout)
            if score < current_best: current_base=best_a
            # if K==n and beta high, final direct
```

Now, `project_simplex` correct:
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
    n=len(v); if n==0 return v; v=np.asarray(v,float); w=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cumsum=np.cumsum(w); idx=np.arange(1,n+1); theta=(cumsum-S)/idx; valid=theta<=w; rho=idx[valid][-1] if any else n; theta=(cumsum[rho-1]-S)/rho; return max(v-theta,0)
```

  size=2n-1; c=fft_convolle_self(a) # using short FFT

Then top gradient function:
```
def lse_toppk_value_grad(a, beta, top_k):
    c=fft_convolve_self_short(a)
    size=len(c)
    if top_k > size: top_k=size
    if top_k <=0: ...
    idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    vals=c[idx]
    cmax=vals.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(vals-cmax)); total=e.sum(); weights=e/total
    value=cmax+np.log(total)/beta
    grad=np.zeros(len(a), dtype=float)
    # Optional threshold = 1e-12 to skip tiny
    for kk, wgt in zip(idx, weights):
        if wgt < 1e-12: continue
        k=int(kk)
        lo = k - (n-1)
        if lo < 0: lo=0
        hi = k + 1
        if hi > n: hi=n
        if lo >= hi: continue
        start = k - lo
        stop = k - hi
        if stop < 0: seg = a[start::-1]
        else: seg = a[start:stop:-1]
        grad[lo:hi] += (2.0*wgt) * seg
    return value, c, grad
```
Check when stop equals start? If hi=lo+1, start=k, stop=k-hi = k-lo-1 < start, segment length 1. Example k=0, lo=0, hi=1, start=0, stop=-1 -> a[0::-1]=[a0]. good. k=7,n=5: lo=3, hi=5, m=3,4 indices 4,3. start=4, stop=2, a[4:2:-1]=[4,3]. good. k=5,n=5, lo=1, hi=5, start=4, stop=0, a[4:0:-1]=[4,3,2,1]. good.

```
def full_lse_value_grad(a,beta):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1; need=3*n-2; fsize=1 << (need-1).bit_length() if need>1 else 1
    A=fft(a, fsize)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A)); c=c[:size]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    e=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax)); total=float(e.sum())
    if total==0: ...
    w=e/total
    W=fft(w, fsize)
    grad=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
    value=cmax + np.log(total)/float(beta)
    # clean: grad[~finite]=0
    return value, c, grad
```
For n small, need c.size. If c has negative tiny, max okay. `e` sum may be huge? c-cmax≤0, safe. total at least top count. log total ≤ log(2n).

def next_pow2(x):
    if x<=1: return 1

```
def full_lse_val_grad(a,beta):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1; fsize=next_pow2(3*n-2 if n>1 else 1)
    A=fft(a, fsize)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A)); c=c[:size]; ...
    cmax=float(c.max())
    e=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax)); total=float(e.sum())
    if total <=0 or not finite: return cmax, c, zeros
    w=e/total
    W=fft(w, fsize)
    grad=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
    grad[~np.isfinite(grad)]=0
    value=cmax + float(np.log(total))/beta
    return value, c, grad
```

    a = normalize(a_init); n=len(a); S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; it=0; start=time.monotonic(); best=y.copy(); best_score=inf
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it+=1
       c=fft_conv_self(y); cur=float(c.max())

       if tot < 1e-30: break
       weights=vals/tot
       grad=direct_grad_weights(y, idx, weights)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm < 1e-15: break
       step = (2.0*S/(n*(1.0+0.001*it))) * step_factor
       x = project_simplex(y - step*grad/gnorm, S)
       if it%20==0:
          cx=fft_conv_self(x); cmax=...

       a=x; t=t_new
       if rng and it %2000==0: perturb y
    return best, best_score
```

Now, `softmax_pipeline` implementation details:
```
def softmax_pipeline(base, time_budget, deadline, label, try_full=False):
    base = safe_normalize(base)
    n=len(base)
    base_score = exact_score(base)
    cur=base.copy(); cur_score=base_score
    print...
    # choose K list based on n and budget
    Ks = []
    for K in [4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096,8192]:
       if K <= n: Ks.append(K)
    # if want full direct, ensure last n with direct model. For K=n, run direct.
    # stages: for K in Ks (excluding n maybe), beta=20; last few beta=80; for K=n, beta=30 and 100 if time.
    stages=[]
    for i,K in enumerate(Ks):
       if K == n:
          stages.append((K, 30, 'high'))
          stages.append((K, 100, 'veryhigh'))
       else:
          if i < len(Ks)//2: stages.append((K, 15))
          else: stages.append((K, 50))
    # To limit total, choose per_stage = max(10, time_budget / (len(stages)+2))
    for K,beta in stages:

    return cur, cur_score

def make_tilt(base, alpha, kind=0):

Now, code details for `safe_normalize`:
```
def safe_normalize(a, target_sum=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1) # but length? target?
    a[~np.isfinite(a)]=0
    np.clip(a,0,1000,aout=a)
    s=a.sum()
    if target_sum is None: n=a.size; target_sum=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, target_sum/a.size)
    a *= target_sum/s
    np.clip(a,0,1000,aout=a)
    # after clip sum changed; renormalize
    s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: ...; a*=target_sum/s
    return a
```
Clipping to 1000 may reduce sum; renormalize can push >1000 if one element clipped? But sum S ~128, max <=S <1000 for n>=1? For n=1, S=1.414 <1000. So after clip fine. For length small, max <S<1000. So one clip enough. But if input had huge 1000, normalization might make max <=S? safe. Use final clip.

`exact_score_a(a)` uses safe_normalize then c. But safe_normalize copies; for speed in loops, assume positive normalized. Still use.

`make_sqrt(n)` recurrence:
```
def make_sqrt(n):
    c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n): c[k]=c[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k)
    c=safe_normalize(c)
    return c
```
This returns sum S. For n=1, c[0]=S.

Could make `make_sqrt_rev` = sqrt[::-1] normalized? Reversal sum same. We can just `sqrt[::-1]`.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic(); absolute_end=start+float(budget_s)
    workspace_deadline=absolute_end-180.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a=None; best_score=np.inf

    # given
    given=None
    try: given=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float).reshape(-1)
    except: given=None
    if given is not None and given.size>=32:
       best_a,best_score,upd=consider_candidate(given,best_a,best_score,"provided")

    # candidates lengths
    n_given = len(given) if given is not None and len(given)>=64 else 8192
    n_main = n_given if 1024 <= n_given <= 16384 else 8192
    sqrt_main = make_sqrt(n_main)
    best_a,best_score,upd=consider_candidate(sqrt_main,best_a,best_score,"sqrt main")
    # add tilts
    best_a,best_score=add_base_tilts(sqrt_main,best_a,best_score,"tilt sqrt")
    if given is not None:
       given_norm=safe_normalize(given)
       if given_norm.size != n_main: # maybe not
          best_a,best_score=add_base_tilts(given_norm,best_a,best_score,"tilt given")
       else:
          best_a,best_score=add_base_tilts(given_norm,best_a,best_score,"tilt given")
    # maybe other lengths quick sqrt
    for n in [4096,16384]: if n != n_main and now<...:
       s=make_sqrt(n); consider; tilts maybe fewer

    # Stage: direct polish current best
    if best_a is not None and time<workspace_deadline-30:
       n=len(best_a)
       timeout=min(90, max(10, (workspace_deadline-time.monotonic())*0.15))
       res=run_softmax_lbfgs(best_a, n, beta=50, timeout=timeout, x0=None, direct=True, label="direct polish 50")
       best_a,best_score,upd=consider_candidate(res[0],best_a,best_score,"direct0")
       # second beta 150
       if time<...:
          timeout=min(90,...)
          res=run_softmax_lbfgs(best_a,n,beta=150,timeout,...,direct=True)
          ...

    # Pipelines

    if given is not None and given_norm.size==n_main and given_norm.size>=64: pipeline_baselines.append((given_norm, "given"))
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a)==n_main: pipeline_baselines.append((best_a, "best"))
    # If best length different, skip.

       rem=workspace_deadline - time.monotonic()

       frac = weights[idx%len(weights)] if idx < len(weights) else 0.2
       budget = min(rem*0.45, total_pipe_budget*weights?) Hmm.

    base=safe_normalize(base); n=len(base); cur=base.copy(); cur_score=exact_score(base)

    if Ks is None: generate [4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096] <= n, plus n if time
    # If n large maybe limit Ks to <=2048 unless final. For K=n, direct expensive; include only near end.
    stages=[]
    # create stages: for all K<n: beta based; for K=n: beta 80,150
    Ks_no_full=[k for k in Ks if k<n]
    for K in Ks_no_full: stages.append((K, choose_beta(K,n)))
    if n <= 20000: # direct
       stages.append((n, 60))
       stages.append((n, 150))
    # Limit total stages if budget small
    if budget < 120: keep first 4 + full maybe
    total_stages=len(stages)
    for i,(K,beta) in enumerate(stages):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 20: break
       # give each stage roughly budget/total, but at least 10
       stage_timeout = min(budget - (i*budget/total_stages)? simpler: max(10, budget/total_stages)
       timeout = min(stage_timeout, rem - 10)
       if timeout < 8: stage_timeout=10
       if timeout < 5: break
       res=run_softmax_lbfgs(cur, K, beta, timeout, x0=None, direct=(K>=n), label=...)
       if res[1] < cur_score - 1e-12:
          cur=res[0].copy(); cur_score=res[1]
          print(...)
    return cur, cur_score

Need `direct_grad_weights` used in Nesterov. Let's implement:
```
def direct_grad_weights(a, idx, weights):
    n=len(a); grad=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k, wgt in zip(idx, weights):
       if wgt <= 1e-15: continue
       k=int(k)
       lo = k - (n - 1)
       if lo < 0: lo=0
       hi = k + 1
       if hi > n: hi=n
       if lo >= hi: continue
       start = k - lo
       stop = k - hi
       if stop < 0:
          segment = a[start::-1]
       else:
          segment = a[start:stop:-1]
       grad[lo:hi] += (2.0*wgt) * segment
    return grad
```
Check if `stop` equals 0, slice `a[start:0:-1]` excludes 0, desired indices down to 1 if k-hi=0. Example k=5,n=6? lo=0, hi=6? stop=-1 None. Let's test an edge where stop=0: n=5,k=4, lo=0, hi=5, start=4, stop=-1 -> segment 4,3,2,1,0. desired grad m0..4 indices 4,3,2,1,0. good stop negative. k=4,n=6: lo=0, hi=5, start=4, stop=-1, segment 4..0; desired m0..4 indices4..0. good. k=6,n=6: lo=1, hi=6, start=5, stop=0, slice 5:0:-1 gives 5,4,3,2,1; desired m=1..5 indices 5,4,3,2,1. correct. Good.

```
def top_subgradient(a,c,top_k):
   idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]; vals=c[idx]; s=float(vals.sum()); if s<=0: return zeros; w=vals/s; return direct_grad_weights(a,idx,w)
```

`run_nesterov_top` details:
```
def run_nesterov_top(a_init, max_time, top_k_init=8, top_k_max=128, rng=None, step_factor=1.0):
    a=safe_normalize(a_init); n=len(a); S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    if n<2: return a, exact_score(a)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; it=0; start=time.monotonic(); best=a.copy(); best_score=exact_score(a)
    max_it = max(100000, int(max_time*1000))
    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time and it<max_it:
       it+=1
       c=fft_conv_self(y); cur=float(c.max())
       if cur<best_score: best_score=cur; best=y.copy()
       top_k=min(top_k_max, top_k_init+it//2000)
       grad=top_subgradient(y,c,top_k)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm < 1e-15: break
       step = (2.0*S/(n*(1.0+0.001*it))) * step_factor
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad/gnorm, S)
       if it % 50 == 0:
          cx=fft_conv_self(x); mx=float(cx.max())
          if mx < best_score: best_score=mx; best=x.copy()
       t_new = 0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-y)
       y = np.maximum(y,0.0)
       y = project_simplex(y, S)
       a=x; t=t_new
       if rng is not None and it % 2000 == 0:
           y = project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0, 0.003*S/np.sqrt(n), n), S)
           t=1.0
    return best,best_score
```
Need `np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t)` could overflow t large? t ~it, it 100k => t=100k, 4e10 fine sqrt 200k. Good.

Maybe FISTA with y momentum using x-y after y projected? Standard FISTA: y = x + ((t_old-1)/t_new)(x - a_old). We set `a=x` after computing y from old y and x? In code, `y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-y)` where y is old y (not a_old). Standard uses previous y? Actually FISTA update: x_k = prox(... y_{k-1}); t_k; y_k = x_k + ((t_{k-1}-1)/t_k)(x_k - x_{k-1}). We used y as old y, not x_prev. Previous code used y = x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new - a) where a was previous x. Need maintain `a_prev`. Let's implement correctly:
```
y=a.copy(); x_prev=a.copy(); t=1
loop:
 x = project...
 t_new...
 y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x - x_prev)
 y=project(y,S)
 x_prev=x; t=t_new
```
Use that. Good.

Top Nesterov step maybe too small; step_factor param. We can use `step = 2.0 * step_factor / (1.0 + 0.01*it) * (S/n?)`? Previous formula 2S/(n*(1+0.005it)). For n=8192, S=128, 2S/n=0.031. Good. Use step_factor 2.0 maybe 0.062 initial. Could be okay. We'll set default step_factor=1.5.

```
def ws_reconstruct(y):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).copy()
    y=np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    ym = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(ym)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: e=np.zeros(len(y)); e[np.argmax(y)]=1; s=1
    x = WS['S'] * e / s
    if WS.get('direct',False): return x, x
    B=WS['B']; scale=x/B; a=WS['base']*scale[WS['block_id']]
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    return a, x
```

Now, `make_tilt` with kind:
```
def make_tilt(base, alpha, kind=0):
    n=len(base); if n==1: return safe_normalize(base)
    t=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/(n-1)
    if kind==0: f=1.0+alpha*t
    elif kind==1: f=np.exp(alpha*t)
    elif kind==2: f=1.0+alpha*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t)
    else: f=1.0
    return safe_normalize(base*f)
```
If kind 2 with alpha negative? f may >? fine. For alpha -0.8 sin can be 0.2 positive; safe clip.

```
def run_topk_polish(a, timeout, top_k=64, rng=None):
  best=a; best_s=score
  step=0.02; y=a
  while time:
     c=conv(y); cur=s; top grad
     if step too small break
     x=project(y-step*grad/gnorm,S)
     cx=conv(x); xs=...
     if xs < cur - 1e-12:
        y=x; step=min(step*1.2, 0.2); update best
     else:
        step*=0.7
        # maybe try random small perturbation if many failures
  return best
```

Now, let's write `run_block_constant_search`:
```
def run_block_constant_search(n, budget, deadline, rng, x0_base=None):
    best_a=None; best_s=inf
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    Ks=[2,3,4,5,8,9,12,16,24,32,48,64]
    Ks=[k for k in Ks if k < n]
    if len(Ks)>8: Ks=Ks[:8]
    stages=[]
    for K in Ks:
       stages.append((K,30,'uni'))
       if x0_base is not None and K <= 16:
          stages.append((K,0,'sqrt'))
    # random starts for K=3,9 maybe
    if rng is not None:
       for K in [3,9,16]: if K<n: stages.append((K,0,'rand'))
    # Dedup maybe
    total=len(stages)
    per = max(8, budget/total)
    for K,beta,kind in stages:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem<10: break
       timeout=min(per, rem-5)
       if timeout<5: timeout=5
       base=np.ones(n)
       x0=None
       if kind=='sqrt' and x0_base is not None:
          block_id=(np.arange(n)*K)//n
          B=np.bincount(block_id, weights=x0_base, minlength=K)
          x0=B
       elif kind=='rand':
          x0=rng.random(K)+0.1
       res=run_softmax_lbfgs(base,K,beta,timeout,x0=x0, label=f"bc K={K} {kind}")
       best_a,best_s,upd=consider_candidate(res[0],best_a,best_s,label)
    return best_a,best_s
```

def choose_beta_for_K(K,n):
    if K <= max(4, n//256): return 10.0
    if K <= max(16, n//64): return 20.0
    if K <= max(64, n//16): return 50.0
    return 100.0

def run_softmax_lbfgs(base, K, beta, timeout, x0=None, label="", direct_hint=False):
    if base is None or len(base)==0: return None, inf
    base=safe_normalize(base)
    n=len(base); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    if K is None or K>=n or direct_hint:
       direct=True; K=n
    else:

    if K < 1: K=1
    if direct:

       init = safe_normalize(init)
       floor_x = max(1e-300, 1e-8*S/n)
       init = np.maximum(init, floor_x); init *= S/init.sum()

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max_conv / (sum(a))**2

The core ideas are:

- Work on the simplex sum(a) = sqrt(2n), so the objective is just the
  maximum coefficient of a are self-convolution.
- Use the analytic square-root profile (1-x)^-1/2 truncated as a strong
  base.  It already has almost flat left convolution coefficients.
- Smoothly optimize low-to-high dimensional parameterizations:
    * 3 / 5 / 9 / 16 ... block-constant sequences
    * block-scaled versions of a base profile (multiresolution)
    * full-dimensional polynomial softmax polish
- Smooth the nonsmooth max with log-sum-exp and use L-BFGS-B.
- Fall back to exact top-lag projected-gradient polishing and keep the
  best exact sequence found.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


WS = {}


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    S = float(S)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    th = (cum - S) / idx
    valid = th <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])) + 1
    else:
        rho = n
    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def safe_normalize(a, target_sum=None):
    """Clip to valid evaluator domain and scale to target L1 norm."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)

    n = a.size
    if target_sum is None:
        target_sum = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-14:
        a = np.full(n, target_sum / n, dtype=np.float64)
        return a

    a *= target_sum / s
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)

    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 < 1e-14:
        a = np.full(n, target_sum / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a *= target_sum / s2
        np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)

    return a


def exact_score(a):
    """Exact internal objective after normalization to sum=sqrt(2n)."""
    a = safe_normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_conv_self(a)
    return float(np.max(c))


def make_sqrt(n):
    """Truncated coefficients of (1-x)^-1/2, normalized."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        c[k] = c[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
    return safe_normalize(c)


def ensure_positive(a):
    """Give a strictly positive base profile, mostly preserving a."""
    a = safe_normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    amax = float(a.max())
    if amax <= 0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    floor = max(1e-12, 1e-8 * amax)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    return safe_normalize(a)


def make_tilt(base, alpha, kind=0):
    """Simple low-dimensional tilts of a base profile."""
    n = len(base)
    if n <= 1:
        return safe_normalize(base)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    if kind == 0:
        f = 1.0 + float(alpha) * t
    elif kind == 1:
        f = np.exp(float(alpha) * t)
    elif kind == 2:
        f = 1.0 + float(alpha) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    else:
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return safe_normalize(base * f)


def consider_candidate(a, best_a, best_score, label=""):
    """Normalize and accept a candidate if it improves the local best."""
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_score, False
    try:
        a = safe_normalize(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            return best_a, best_score, False
        score = exact_score(a)
    except Exception:
        return best_a, best_score, False

    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-13:
        if best_a is None:
            print("[improve] first %s score=%.10f len=%d" % (label, score, a.size))
        else:
            print("[improve] %s score=%.10f len=%d" % (label, score, a.size))
        return a.copy(), float(score), True
    return best_a, best_score, False


def fft_conv_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution, non-negative cleaned."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = next_pow2(size)
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(irfft(A * A, fsize))
    c = c[:size]
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def full_lse_val_grad(a, beta):
    """
    Log-sum-exp of all convolution coefficients and its gradient wrt a.
    Uses one padded FFT for both convolution and the cross-correlation
    needed for gradient.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    size = 2 * n - 1
    need = 3 * n - 2
    if need < 1:
        need = 1
    fsize = next_pow2(need)

    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(irfft(A * A, fsize))
    c = c[:size]
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta < 1e-8:
        beta = 1e-8

    cmax = float(c.max())
    e = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
    total = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        e[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        total = 1.0

    w = e / total
    W = rfft(w, fsize)
    grad = 2.0 * np.real(irfft(W * np.conj(A), fsize))
    grad = grad[:n]
    grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0

    value = cmax + float(np.log(total)) / beta
    return value, cmax, grad


def direct_grad_from_top_lags(a, idx, weights):
    """
    Gradient sum_m sum_k weights[k] * d c_k / d a_m for selected lags.
    Direct O(n * #lags) implementation, good for small #active lags.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, wt in zip(idx, weights):
        wt = float(wt)
        if wt <= 1e-16:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        lo = k - (n - 1)
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = k + 1
        if hi > n:
            hi = n
        if lo >= hi:
            continue
        start = k - lo
        stop = k - hi
        if stop < 0:
            seg = a[start::-1]
        else:
            seg = a[start:stop:-1]
        grad[lo:hi] += (2.0 * wt) * seg
    return grad


def top_subgradient(a, c, top_k):
    """Subgradient using weights proportional to the largest lags."""
    n = len(a)
    if top_k <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    size = len(c)
    if size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    top_k = min(top_k, size)
    idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    vals = c[idx]
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = vals / s
    return direct_grad_from_top_lags(a, idx, w)


def run_nesterov_top(a_init, max_time, top_k_init=8, top_k_max=128,
                     step_factor=1.5, rng=None):
    """Accelerated projected subgradient method on exact top-lag gradient."""
    a = safe_normalize(a_init)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 64:
        return a, exact_score(a)

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    y = a.copy()
    x_prev = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    it = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = exact_score(best)

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        c = fft_conv_self(y)
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best = y.copy()

        top_k = min(top_k_max, top_k_init + it // 2000)
        grad = top_subgradient(y, c, top_k)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        step = (2.0 * S / (float(n) * (1.0 + 0.001 * it))) * float(step_factor)
        x = project_simplex(y - step * grad / gnorm, S)

        if it % 50 == 0:
            cx = fft_conv_self(x)
            mx = float(cx.max())
            if mx < best_score:
                best_score = mx
                best = x.copy()

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - x_prev)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        y = project_simplex(y, S)

        x_prev = x
        t = t_new

        if rng is not None and it % 2000 == 0:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-4, 0.003 * S / np.sqrt(n)), n)
            y = project_simplex(y + noise, S)
            t = 1.0

    return best, float(best_score)


def run_topk_polish(a_init, max_time, top_k_init=16, top_k_max=128, rng=None):
    """Monotone projected subgradient polishing; every accepted step lowers exact score."""
    a = safe_normalize(a_init)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 64:
        return a, exact_score(a)

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    y = a.copy()
    best = y.copy()
    best_score = exact_score(best)
    step = 0.03
    fails = 0
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time and step > 1e-7:
        c = fft_conv_self(y)
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best = y.copy()

        top_k = min(top_k_max, top_k_init + int((time.monotonic() - start) * 0.2))
        grad = top_subgradient(y, c, top_k)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        x = project_simplex(y - step * grad / gnorm, S)
        cx = fft_conv_self(x)
        xs = float(cx.max())

        if xs < cur - 1e-13:
            y = x
            step = min(0.25, step * 1.25)
            fails = 0
            if xs < best_score:
                best_score = xs
                best = y.copy()
        else:
            step *= 0.72
            fails += 1
            if fails == 200 and rng is not None:
                y = project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.002 * S / np.sqrt(n), n), S)
                step = min(0.1, max(step, 0.01))
                fails = 0
            if fails > 2000:
                break

    return best, float(best_score)


def ws_reconstruct(y):
    """Reconstruct sequence a and block sums x from softmax log-parameters y."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    y[~np.isfinite(y)] = 0.0
    y -= float(np.mean(y))

    ym = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(ym)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros_like(y)
        e[int(np.argmax(y))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0

    x = WS["S"] * e / s

    if WS.get("direct", False):
        return x, x

    B = WS["B"]
    scale = x / B
    a = WS["base"] * scale[WS["block_id"]]
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    return a, x


def ws_objective(y):
    """L-BFGS-B objective: smooth LSE of max convolution under softmax."""
    if not WS:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if WS.get("stop", False):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(y), dtype=np.float64)

    cnt = int(WS.get("eval_count", 0)) + 1
    WS["eval_count"] = cnt
    if cnt > int(WS.get("max_evals", 10 ** 9)) or time.monotonic() > float(WS.get("deadline", time.monotonic() + 1.0)):
        WS["stop"] = True
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(y), dtype=np.float64)

    a, x = ws_reconstruct(y)
    value, cmax, grad_a = full_lse_val_grad(a, float(WS.get("beta", 20.0)))

    if cmax < float(WS.get("best_score", np.inf)) - 1e-14:
        WS["best_score"] = float(cmax)
        WS["best_a"] = a.copy()

    if WS.get("direct", False):
        gx = grad_a
    else:
        gx = np.bincount(WS["block_id"], weights=grad_a * WS["base"], minlength=int(WS["K"]))
        gx = gx / WS["B"]

    gx[~np.isfinite(gx)] = 0.0
    gx = np.asarray(gx, dtype=np.float64)
    drop = float(np.dot(gx, x)) / float(WS["S"])
    gy = x * (gx - drop)
    gy[~np.isfinite(gy)] = 0.0

    return float(value), gy


def ws_callback(xk, *args):
    try:
        if WS.get("stop", False) or time.monotonic() > float(WS.get("deadline", time.monotonic() + 1.0)):
            WS["stop"] = True
            return True
    except Exception:
        pass
    return False


def run_softmax_lbfgs(base, K, beta, timeout, x0=None, label="", direct_hint=False):
    """
    Optimize sequence in a softmax-simplex parameterization.

    If K >= n or direct_hint, the model is the full sequence x.
    Otherwise a = base * (x / block_sum(base)) within each of K contiguous blocks.
    """
    base = safe_normalize(base)
    n = len(base)
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    beta = float(beta)
    K = int(K)

    if K < 1:
        K = 1

    if direct_hint or K >= n:
        direct = True
        K = n
        base = None
        B = None
        block_id = None
        if x0 is None:
            init_x = safe_normalize(base if base is not None else np.ones(n))
        else:
            init_x = safe_normalize(x0, S)
    else:
        direct = False
        base = ensure_positive(base)
        block_id = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64) * K) // n
        B = np.bincount(block_id, weights=base, minlength=K).astype(np.float64)
        B = np.where(B > 0.0, B, 1e-12 * S / K)
        if x0 is None:
            init_x = B.copy()
        else:
            init_x = safe_normalize(x0, S)

    floor_x = max(1e-300, 1e-8 * S / float(K))
    init_x = np.asarray(init_x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    init_x = safe_normalize(init_x, S)
    init_x = np.maximum(init_x, floor_x)
    init_x *= S / float(init_x.sum())

    y0 = np.log(init_x / S)
    y0 -= float(np.mean(y0))

    initial_a, _ = ws_reconstruct(y0)
    init_score = exact_score(initial_a)

    WS.clear()
    WS["n"] = n
    WS["S"] = S
    WS["K"] = K
    WS["direct"] = direct
    if direct:
        WS["base"] = None
        WS["B"] = None
        WS["block_id"] = None
    else:
        WS["base"] = base
        WS["B"] = B
        WS["block_id"] = block_id
    WS["beta"] = beta
    WS["deadline"] = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(timeout))
    WS["max_evals"] = int(min(200000, max(1000, float(timeout) * 500.0)))
    WS["eval_count"] = 0
    WS["stop"] = False
    WS["best_score"] = float(init_score)
    WS["best_a"] = initial_a.copy()
    WS["best_y"] = y0.copy()

    if minimize is None:
        return initial_a.copy(), float(init_score)

    if K == 1:
        return initial_a.copy(), float(init_score)

    max_evals = int(WS["max_evals"])
    opts = {
        "maxiter": max(100, int(0.25 * max_evals)),
        "maxfev": max_evals,
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "maxcor": min(20, max(2, K - 1)),
        "maxls": 8,
        "disp": False,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(ws_objective, y0, method="L-BFGS-B",
                       jac=True, callback=ws_callback, options=opts)
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            ya, _ = ws_reconstruct(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
            sc = exact_score(ya)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < WS["best_score"] - 1e-14:
                WS["best_score"] = float(sc)
                WS["best_a"] = ya.copy()
    except Exception as e:
        print("[softmax lbfgs] %s stopped: %s" % (label, e.__class__.__name__))

    out_a = WS.get("best_a", initial_a.copy())
    out_a = safe_normalize(out_a)
    out_s = exact_score(out_a)
    if not np.isfinite(out_s):
        out_a, out_s = initial_a, init_score
    return out_a.copy(), float(out_s)


def choose_beta_for_K(K, n):
    K = int(K)
    if K <= max(4, n // 256):
        return 10.0
    if K <= max(16, n // 64):
        return 20.0
    if K <= max(64, n // 16):
        return 50.0
    return 100.0


def softmax_pipeline(base, budget, deadline, label=""):
    """Coarse-to-fine block scaling pipeline, ending with direct full polish."""
    base = safe_normalize(base)
    n = len(base)
    if n == 0 or budget <= 0:
        return base, exact_score(base)

    cur = base.copy()
    cur_score = exact_score(cur)
    print("[pipe] start %s len=%d score=%.10f budget=%.1fs" %
          (label, n, cur_score, budget))

    Ks = [2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096]
    Ks = [k for k in Ks if k < n]
    stages = []
    for K in Ks:
        stages.append((K, choose_beta_for_K(K, n)))
    if n <= 32768:
        stages.append((n, 60.0))
        stages.append((n, 150.0))

    if len(stages) == 0:
        if n <= 32768:
            stages.append((n, 80.0))
        else:
            return cur, cur_score

    # If budget is tiny, keep only a few small stages plus final direct if possible.
    if budget < 120.0:
        reduced = []
        for K, b in stages:
            if K < n and K <= 64:
                reduced.append((K, b))
        if n <= 32768 and len(reduced) < 3:
            reduced.append((n, 80.0))
        if reduced:
            stages = reduced

    total = float(max(1, len(stages)))
    for i, (K, beta) in enumerate(stages):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 5.0:
            break

        rem = deadline - now
        per_stage = max(8.0, budget / (total - i) if i < total else 8.0)
        timeout = min(per_stage, rem - 8.0)
        if timeout < 6.0:
            timeout = min(15.0, rem - 2.0)
        if timeout < 4.0:
            break

        res_a, res_s = run_softmax_lbfgs(
            cur, K, beta, timeout,
            x0=None,
            label="%s K=%d beta=%g" % (label, K, beta)
        )
        if res_a is not None and np.isfinite(res_s) and res_s < cur_score - 1e-13:
            cur = res_a.copy()
            cur_score = float(res_s)
            print("[pipe] %s K=%d beta=%g score=%.10f" % (label, K, beta, cur_score))

    return cur, float(cur_score)


def run_block_constant_search(n, budget, deadline, rng=None, x0_base=None):
    """Search over contiguous block-constant sequences with 2,3,5,9,16,... blocks."""
    if n < 16 or budget <= 0:
        return None, np.inf

    best_a = None
    best_s = np.inf

    Ks = [2, 3, 4, 5, 8, 9, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64]
    Ks = [k for k in Ks if k < n]
    if len(Ks) > 10:
        Ks = Ks[:10]

    stages = []
    for K in Ks:
        stages.append((K, 30.0, "uni"))
        if x0_base is not None and K <= 16:
            stages.append((K, 30.0, "sqrt"))

    if rng is not None:
        for K in [3, 9, 16]:
            if K < n:
                stages.append((K, 30.0, "rand"))

    if len(stages) > 14:
        stages = stages[:14]

    total = float(max(1, len(stages)))
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for i, (K, beta, kind) in enumerate(stages):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        per = max(8.0, budget / (total - i) if i < total else 8.0)
        timeout = min(per, deadline - now - 8.0)
        if timeout < 6.0:
            timeout = min(15.0, deadline - now - 2.0)
        if timeout < 4.0:
            break

        x0 = None
        if kind == "sqrt" and x0_base is not None:
            block_id = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64) * K) // n
            B = np.bincount(block_id, weights=x0_base, minlength=K).astype(np.float64)
            x0 = B
        elif kind == "rand" and rng is not None:
            x0 = rng.random(K, dtype=np.float64) + 0.05

        res_a, res_s = run_softmax_lbfgs(
            ones, K, beta, timeout,
            x0=x0,
            label="blockconst K=%d %s" % (K, kind)
        )
        best_a, best_s, _ = consider_candidate(res_a, best_a, best_s,
                                                "blockconst K=%d %s" % (K, kind))

    return best_a, float(best_s)


def add_base_tilts(base, best_a, best_score, label, max_evals=55):
    """Very fast one-dimensional shape search around a base profile."""
    if base is None or len(base) == 0:
        return best_a, best_score

    n = len(base)
    if n > 16384:
        alphas = np.linspace(-0.8, 2.4, 7)
    else:
        alphas = np.linspace(-0.8, 3.0, 11)

    for kind in (0, 1, 2):
        for alpha in alphas:
            best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(
                make_tilt(base, alpha, kind),
                best_a,
                best_score,
                "%s tilt k=%d a=%.3f" % (label, kind, float(alpha))
            )
            if best_score < 1.4995:
                return best_a, best_score

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    absolute_end = start + max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    # Conservative: do not start new expensive stages within last 150s.
    workspace_deadline = absolute_end - 150.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    print("propose_candidate start budget=%.1fs" % float(budget_s))

    # ---------- Provided previous SOTA ----------
    given = None
    try:
        given = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        given = None

    given_norm = None
    if given is not None and given.size >= 32:
        given_norm = safe_normalize(given)
        best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(given_norm, best_a, best_score, "provided")

    # ---------- Primary length ----------
    n_main = 8192
    if given_norm is not None and 1024 <= given_norm.size <= 16384:
        n_main = int(given_norm.size)

    sqrt_main = make_sqrt(n_main)
    best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(sqrt_main, best_a, best_score, "sqrt %d" % n_main)
    best_a, best_score = add_base_tilts(sqrt_main, best_a, best_score, "sqrt")

    if given_norm is not None and given_norm.size == n_main:
        best_a, best_score = add_base_tilts(given_norm, best_a, best_score, "given")

    # A few alternate lengths, just quick square-root candidates.
    for n_alt in (4096, 16384):
        if n_alt == n_main:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > workspace_deadline - 120.0:
            break
        s = make_sqrt(n_alt)
        best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(s, best_a, best_score, "sqrt %d" % n_alt)

    # ---------- Block-constant 3/9/16 segment search ----------
    if time.monotonic() < workspace_deadline - 90.0:
        bc_budget = min(280.0, max(60.0, (workspace_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.22))
        bc_a, bc_s = run_block_constant_search(
            n_main, bc_budget, workspace_deadline, rng=rng, x0_base=sqrt_main
        )
        best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(bc_a, best_a, best_score, "blockconst best")
        blockconst_a = bc_a
    else:
        blockconst_a = None

    # ---------- Direct full-dimensional polish of current global best ----------
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < workspace_deadline - 60.0:
        n_best = len(best_a)
        rem = workspace_deadline - time.monotonic()
        t1 = min(120.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.20))
        res_a, res_s = run_softmax_lbfgs(
            best_a, n_best, 60.0, t1,
            x0=None, direct_hint=True, label="direct polish 60"
        )
        best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(res_a, best_a, best_score, "direct 60")

        if time.monotonic() < workspace_deadline - 40.0:
            t2 = min(100.0, max(15.0, (workspace_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.15))
            res_a, res_s = run_softmax_lbfgs(
                best_a, len(best_a), 150.0, t2,
                x0=None, direct_hint=True, label="direct polish 150"
            )
            best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(res_a, best_a, best_score, "direct 150")

        # Small randomized direct restart.
        if time.monotonic() < workspace_deadline - 30.0 and rng is not None:
            a2 = safe_normalize(best_a * (1.0 + 0.02 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, len(best_a))))
            t3 = min(80.0, max(15.0, (workspace_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.12))
            res_a, res_s = run_softmax_lbfgs(
                a2, len(a2), 80.0, t3,
                x0=None, direct_hint=True, label="direct random restart"
            )
            best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(res_a, best_a, best_score, "direct rand")

    # ---------- Coarse-to-fine pipelines from several bases ----------
    baselines = []
    if sqrt_main is not None:
        baselines.append(("sqrt", sqrt_main))
    if blockconst_a is not None and len(blockconst_a) == n_main:
        baselines.append(("blockconst", blockconst_a))
    if given_norm is not None and given_norm.size == n_main:
        baselines.append(("given", given_norm))
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) == n_main:
        baselines.append(("best", best_a))

    # Deduplicate by identity-ish score/shape? Keep few.
    if len(baselines) > 4:
        baselines = baselines[:4]

    if baselines and time.monotonic() < workspace_deadline - 80.0:
        allowed_until = workspace_deadline - 120.0
        pipe_total = max(60.0, allowed_until - time.monotonic())
        weights = {"sqrt": 0.35, "blockconst": 0.25, "given": 0.20, "best": 0.20,
                   "other": 0.1}
        chosen = []
        for lab, arr in baselines:
            chosen.append((lab, arr, weights.get(lab, 0.15)))
        tot_w = sum(w for _, _, w in chosen)

        for lab, arr, w in chosen:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > allowed_until - 30.0:
                break
            budget = max(30.0, pipe_total * (w / max(1e-12, tot_w)))
            budget = min(budget, allowed_until - now - 20.0)
            if budget < 25.0:
                continue

            res_a, res_s = softmax_pipeline(arr, budget, allowed_until, label=lab)
            best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(res_a, best_a, best_score, "pipeline " + lab)

    # ---------- Final local polishing with exact top-lag methods ----------
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < workspace_deadline - 20.0:
        rem = max(0.0, workspace_deadline - time.monotonic())
        t = min(180.0, rem * 0.55)
        if t > 10.0:
            pa, ps = run_nesterov_top(best_a, t, top_k_init=16, top_k_max=128,
                                      step_factor=1.6, rng=rng)
            best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(pa, best_a, best_score, "nesterov polish")

        rem = max(0.0, workspace_deadline - time.monotonic())
        t = min(120.0, rem * 0.5)
        if t > 8.0:
            pa, ps = run_topk_polish(best_a, t, top_k_init=24, top_k_max=96, rng=rng)
            best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(pa, best_a, best_score, "topk polish")

    # ---------- Optional longer square-root exploration if lots of time left ----------
    if time.monotonic() < workspace_deadline - 120.0 and n_main != 16384:
        n_long = 16384 if n_main < 16384 else 32768
        if n_long <= 32768:
            rem = max(0.0, workspace_deadline - time.monotonic())
            budget = min(360.0, rem * 0.25)
            if budget > 80.0:
                s_long = make_sqrt(n_long)
                best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(s_long, best_a, best_score, "sqrt long")
                a_l, s_l = run_block_constant_search(n_long, min(120.0, budget * 0.5),
                                                     workspace_deadline, rng=rng, x0_base=s_long)
                best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(a_l, best_a, best_score, "blockconst long")

                a_pipe, s_pipe = softmax_pipeline(s_long, min(240.0, budget * 0.5),
                                                  workspace_deadline, label="long sqrt")
                best_a, best_score, _ = consider_candidate(a_pipe, best_a, best_score, "pipeline long")

    # ---------- Final safety and verification ----------
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_sqrt(8192)

    best_a = safe_normalize(best_a)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)]

    # Ensure no NaN/infinity slipped in.
    cleaned = []
    for x in final_list:
        if np.isfinite(x):
            cleaned.append(float(x))
        else:
            cleaned.append(0.0)
    final_list = cleaned
    if len(final_list) == 0:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in make_sqrt(8192)]

    # If the sequence sum is too close to zero under evaluator rules, replace.
    ssum = sum(final_list)
    if ssum < 0.01:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in make_sqrt(8192)]

    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        print("final verification score: %.10f len=%d elapsed=%.1fs" %
              (ver if np.isfinite(ver) else np.inf, len(final_list),
               time.monotonic() - start))
        if not np.isfinite(ver):
            print("evaluate_sequence rejected final; using sqrt fallback")
            final_list = [float(x) for x in make_sqrt(8192)]
    except Exception as e:
        # If evaluate_sequence is unavailable in this environment, return the best we found.
        print("evaluate_sequence unavailable or error:", e.__class__.__name__)

    return final_list
```
